TL;DR: 本文提出ESBMC-LLB，一种将梯形逻辑炸弹（LLB）检测转化为形式化验证问题的方法。通过暴露功能块内部逻辑，利用k-induction进行无界证明和限界模型检测生成反例作为触发器，在Iacobelli 2024数据集上检测全部30个炸弹并恢复所有触发器，在SWaT语料库v1.0.0上检测率99%且零误报。论文还明确了语义模型检测与CFG-triage的互补边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯形图验证器在中间表示（IR）阶段丢弃功能块体，导致隐藏在功能块内部的恶意逻辑（梯形逻辑炸弹）对证明器不可见。此外，现有基于签名、异常和CFG-triage的检测器缺乏触发器的可恢复性保证，且无法应对自适应触发器（计算型、混淆算术型、多扫描型）。需要一种能暴露功能块逻辑、提供无界证明并精确恢复触发条件的新方法。

Method: 基于ESBMC-PLC+验证引擎，添加建模层以暴露功能块内部逻辑。将炸弹检测重新定义为形式化验证问题：(1)利用扫描看门狗暴露非终止型载荷为活性违规；(2)利用输出接线暴露执行器伪造型载荷为安全违规。采用k-induction实现跨所有扫描周期的无界炸弹缺失证明；限界模型检测器（BMC）返回反例即触发器。同时扩展模拟量支持，使SWaT语料库完全可解析。

Result: 在Iacobelli 2024公开数据集上，检测全部30个炸弹并恢复每个触发器，包括CFG-triage无法检测的自适应触发器。在PLC-Defuser的SWaT语料库v1.0.0上，检测149/150个炸弹（99%），零误报，并恢复所有触发器；在后续含非线性非终止炸弹的版本上，因SMT求解器超时，检测率降至49%。

Conclusion: 语义模型检测与CFG-triage是互补技术：前者提供无界证明、自适应触发器鲁棒性，擅长安抚布尔/整数和线性模拟量逻辑；后者擅长安抚非线性模拟量非终止型炸弹。二者各有适用边界，应结合使用。

Abstract: A Ladder Logic Bomb (LLB) is malicious control logic in a Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) program that lies dormant until a trigger activates a payload to manipulate actuators, forge sensor readings, or deny operator control. We observe that real malicious logic hides inside function-block bodies, which existing ladder-diagram verifiers drop from their intermediate representation (IR), making bombs invisible to provers. We present ESBMC-LLB, which uses ESBMC-PLC+ as its verification engine and adds a modeling layer that exposes function-block logic and recasts bomb detection as a formal verification problem: a scan-watchdog exposes non-termination payloads, and output wiring exposes actuator-forgery payloads as safety violations. k-induction gives an unbounded proof of bomb-absence across all scans, and the bounded model checker returns a counterexample that is the trigger - guarantees that signature, anomaly, and CFG-triage detectors lack. On the public Iacobelli 2024 dataset, ESBMC-LLB detects all 30 bombs and recovers every trigger; it also detects adaptive triggers (computed, opaque-arithmetic, multi-scan) that evade CFG-triage. We also report the first semantic model-checker evaluation on PLC-Defuser's SWaT corpus: our analog extension makes the full corpus parseable; on v1.0.0, it detects 149/150 bombs (99%) with zero false positives, recovering each trigger; on a later version with nonlinear non-termination bombs, detection drops to 49% as the SMT solver times out. We conclude that semantic model checking and CFG-triage are complementary - the former gives unbounded proofs, adaptive-trigger robustness, and handles Boolean/integer and linear analog logic; the latter leads to nonlinear analog non-termination, and we delineate where each wins.

</details>


### [107] [Two Axes of LLM Abstention: Answer Correctness and Question Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08456)
*Benedikt J. Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 模型应区分"答错"与"不应答"两类拒绝情形，本文发现单一的置信度评分无法分离这两个维度：答案置信度跟踪答案正确性，但对问题是否可回答几乎视而不见；隐藏状态线性探针则恰好相反。这种盲区不随模型规模增大而缩小，在虚假前提问题（CREPE）上最为严重。作者提出了一种基于两个独立评分的校准策略，将可回答性评分与正确性评分分别阈值化，在保证覆盖率的同时显著优于单阈值策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型需要拒绝两类本质上不同的情况：模型会答错的问题，以及根本不应回答的问题（如无法回答的问题或基于虚假前提的问题）。现有方法通常依赖单一的置信度评分进行阈值拒绝，但这无法区分上述两种情况。本文旨在揭示并量化这一盲区，并提出可操作的解决方案。

Method: 在5个指令微调模型（2B至14B，涵盖三个模型家族）上，作者（1）通过答案置信度与隐藏状态线性探针，系统评估了两个拒绝轴——"答案正确性"与"问题可回答性"的分离程度；（2）在CREPE等虚假前提数据集上测量盲区严重程度，包括P(IK)、P(True)等常用指标；（3）提出基于探针路由的指令策略，替代直接要求模型检查前提的方法；（4）构建双阈值校准策略，将可回答性评分和正确性评分分别设定阈值，形成可认证的拒绝策略，并与单阈值策略进行对比。

Result: 答案置信度几乎无法判断问题是否可回答，而隐藏状态探针可达0.69-0.77 AUROC；直接要求模型检查前提会导致其对正确前提和虚假前提均大量质疑（57%的错误质疑）；通过探针路由同一指令可将质疑精确度提升约三倍。双阈值策略在0.75正确答案覆盖率下可同时认证两个预算（不可回答率与错误率），而单阈值策略仅为0.31；在14B模型上，双阈值策略是唯一能够完成认证的策略。

Conclusion: 模型的"答案正确性"与"问题可回答性"是两个独立轴，单一置信度评分存在系统性盲区。该盲区可通过隐藏状态探针有效弥补。基于探针路由的校准策略能够显著提升拒绝的精确性和可控性，为构建更安全的语言模型部署策略提供了实用框架。

Abstract: A model should refuse two different things: answers it would get wrong, and questions it should not answer at all, such as unanswerable ones or ones resting on a false premise. The usual recipe thresholds a single confidence score, which cannot tell these apart. Across five instruction-tuned models from three families (2B to 14B), we find they are separate axes. Ordinary answer-confidence tracks whether an answer is right but is nearly blind to whether the question is answerable; a linear probe on hidden states does the reverse. The blind spot does not shrink with scale. It is worst on naturally occurring false-premise questions (CREPE). There, answer-confidence, P(IK), P(True), and even asking the model outright whether a premise is false all stay near chance, while a hidden-state probe reaches 0.69 to 0.77 AUROC: the model represents a problem it will not report. This turns out to be fixable. Instructing a model to check premises backfires, because it then disputes sound and false premises alike (57% false challenges), unable to tell them apart; routing the same instruction with the probe roughly triples challenge precision. We turn the two axes into a calibrated policy that answers only when an answerability score and a correctness score each clear a separately certifies behave differently: the unanswerable-answer rate is controllable at every scale, while the wrong-answer rate is capped by model accuracy, so the guarantee tightens as threshold policy certifies both budgets at 0.75 coverage of correct answers, against 0.31 for a single threshold; at 14B it is the only policy that certifies at all.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-seed explainability using Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08499)
*Bendegúz Váradi,Zoltán Kmetty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Procrustes正交对齐的联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器方法，用于从独立训练的BERT模型中提取跨随机种子的通用特征，在三个基准数据集上Pearson相关系数达到0.70以上，优于事后对齐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机制可解释性领域，跨种子特征通用性是一个根本性挑战：由于字典学习是非凸的，独立训练的网络会学到未对齐的特征空间，表面上相同的特征可能因随机初始化而存在实质性差异。现有方法多采用事后对齐，效果有限，亟需一种能使独立训练模型学到对齐特征空间的前置方案。

Method: 提出Procrustes-conditioned联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器（SAE）。具体流程：首先在独立种子训练的BERT激活空间之间计算正交Procrustes旋转，实现空间预对齐；然后在此对齐空间上联合训练SAE，融合Top-K稀疏性约束、端到端下游任务优化，以及用于复活死亡特征的辅助损失函数。

Result: 在五个独立种子对（共十个BERT模型）上，跨越SST-2、Stanford Politeness和TweetEval Emotion三个基准数据集进行评估。完整pipeline在所有三个数据集上产生比事后对齐基线更通用的特征，跨种子的Pearson相关系数达到0.70及以上。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了跨种子特征不对齐问题，通过Procrustes预对齐联合训练得到的特征具有高通用性，且高通用性特征编码了可解释的社会语言学模式，为机制可解释性中特征通用性问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: We present a Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) for extracting cross-seed universal features from independently trained BERT models. Cross-seed feature universality is a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability: because dictionary learning is non-convex, independently trained networks learn misaligned feature spaces, so apparently identical features may differ by random initialization. We address this by computing an orthogonal Procrustes rotation between seeds' activation spaces before joint SAE training, combining Top-K sparsity, end-to-end downstream optimization, and an auxiliary dead-feature revival loss based on previous SAE literature. Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [109] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估器更换会导致LLM-as-judge分数漂移，即使候选回答不变。本文将此视为测量有效性问题，比较了Qwen3密集模型（1.7B→32B）和MiniMax M系列API两种升级路径，发现法官升级不可互换，且更强法官仅部分减轻位置与冗长偏见；结构化辩论可移动判决但归因困难。最终提出报告应包含数据集切片、偏见探针、误差依赖估计和协议审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge评估范式中，评估器模型本身被替换时，分数会发生变化——即使被评估的候选回答完全固定。这种"评估器替换歧义"本质上是一个测量有效性问题，但现有实践对此缺乏系统性分析与规范。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上，对比两种实际可用的法官升级路径：(1) Qwen3密集法官从1.7B扩展到32B参数的缩放路径；(2) MiniMax M2到M2.7发布API的跨版本路径。分析升级的互换性、偏见（位置偏见、冗长偏见）变化、重复采样陪审团的效果，以及结构化辩论对判决的影响。还检验了解析器和回退日志对辩论归因的作用。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 法官升级不可互换——仅Qwen3 1.7B→4B给出稳健的相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本之间未显示此类增益；(2) 更强的法官可减少但无法消除位置偏见和冗长偏见；(3) 当误差存在相关性时，重复采样陪审团几乎不增加价值；(4) 结构化辩论可大幅移动判决，但若无解析器和回退日志，这些变化无法被归因于真正的审慎推理。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge报告应标准化地包含四个要素：数据集切片、偏见探针、误差依赖估计和协议审计轨迹，以确保评估结果的测量有效性、可复现性和可归因性。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [110] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO 提出一种面向 MoE 架构的结构化剪枝框架，通过将自回归的专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用稳态分布捕捉跨层依赖关系，从而得到全局感知的专家重要性启发式。在 50% 压缩率下，该方法在五个领域的平均性能保持率上超越 SOTA 基线最高达 10.61%，且跨任务方差显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 模型虽通过稀疏激活实现高效推理，但完整专家库仍需常驻内存，构成部署瓶颈。当前结构化剪枝方法多针对密集 Transformer 设计，采用局部启发式评估专家重要性，忽视了 MoE 路由的跨层相互依赖特性，导致剪枝效果次优。

Method: MAESTRO 将 MoE 中自回归的专家激活序列建模为遍历马尔可夫链（Ergodic Markov Chain），通过计算马尔可夫链的稳态分布来编码专家跨层依赖关系，由此导出全局路由一致的专家重要性评分，并基于此进行结构化剪枝。

Result: 在 Safety、Bias、Ethics 等五个多样化领域上，MAESTRO 在 50% 严格压缩率下的平均性能保持率超越现有最优基线方法高达 10.61%，同时跨任务方差显著降低。

Conclusion: 全局的、与路由机制一致的剪枝策略能够产生在异构任务上泛化更一致的 MoE 模型。MAESTRO 验证了建模跨层专家转移依赖对 MoE 剪枝的关键价值。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [111] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree 是一种免训练的 best-first 树搜索推测解码方法，利用 Domino 草稿器的条件化、非因子化修正对每个根到节点路径进行评分，通过限制每节点候选 top-M 实现实用化，在多项基准上取得了最高的平均接受长度和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散草稿器（如 DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，而 best-first 树方法（如 DDTree）基于因子化形式扩展候选树，无法表示 Domino 草稿器中基于 GRU 的路径依赖条件修正。作者希望将 Domino 的条件化非因子化修正引入 best-first 树搜索中，以进一步提升推测解码的接受长度和吞吐量。

Method: 提出 DominoTree：一种免训练的 best-first 草稿树方法。核心思路是利用 Domino 草稿器的条件化、非因子化修正沿每个根到节点路径进行评分，并通过将每节点修正限制在候选 top-M 内来降低计算开销，使其实际可行。树构建采用 GPU 原生的 CUDA graph 构建器，与 Python 参考实现位级别一致，保证接受率不变且每轮树构建开销低廉。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 的八个基准上，DominoTree 相比自回归解码实现最高 6.6 倍加速，平均接受长度达每轮 10.7 个 token，在所有测试温度下均为最优。吞吐量方面，在 Qwen3-4B 上整体优于已发布的 Domino 解码器 9-10%，Alpaca 上最高 +22%；在所有温度下均超越 DDTree/CaDDTree。在 Qwen3-8B 上，T=0 时吞吐量超越 DDTree +24%，高温下优势收窄至持平或小幅落后，但总体仍优于 DFlash 和 Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree 成功将 Domino 的路径依赖条件修正引入 best-first 树搜索框架，在无需额外训练的前提下显著提升了推测解码的接受长度和吞吐量，尤其在中低温场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [112] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX 是一个面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用式精炼框架，在删除和修改之外引入插入操作以补全编辑函数空间，通过程序监督生成管道实现细粒度实例级编辑，在提升数据质量和效率的同时保证了大规模执行的稳定性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可用训练数据逼近物理极限，Scaling Laws 的收益递减，LLM 的提升重心从数据扩展转向高质量数据利用。然而，现有大规模语料精炼方法存在显著局限：基于规则的方法受固定启发式约束，难以处理实例级差异；基于 LLM 的方法虽能提升质量，但无法满足大规模数据处理对效率和可靠性的要求。

Method: UltraX 提出函数调用式精炼框架，核心包括：(1) 在删除和修改之外引入插入操作，补全编辑函数空间，实现细粒度实例级编辑；(2) 构建可靠的程序监督生成管道：先通过数据集自适应提示优化引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本，再通过行对齐映射和动态上下文替换将原始-精炼文本对转化为结构化程序监督；(3) 通过低置信度样本过滤和按操作组合的比例控制采样来提升监督质量并稳定训练分布；(4) 推理执行阶段，采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统化后处理对模型输出进行规范化和验证，提升大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，UltraX 在所有语料上取得了最高的平均性能，并且在使用更少训练 token 的情况下达到或超越了基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过函数调用式编辑框架有效解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中的质量、效率和可靠性三重挑战，为后 Scaling Laws 时代的高质量数据利用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [113] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm提出一种渐进式递归委托框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、支持节点自行求解或委派子节点、以及向上返回证据与结果，解决了LLM搜索智能体在深度与广度覆盖上的双重挑战，在多个基准上显著优于单智能体和现有多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct风格的单智能体受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时实现搜索深度与广度；而多智能体系统虽通过并行执行提升了覆盖率，但在递归深度、协作自适应性和基于证据的扩展方面仍存在明显不足。

Method: 提出WebSwarm框架，核心是渐进式递归委托：1）动态实例化搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式；2）节点可自行求解或将任务委托给子节点，求解后向上返回证据和结果；3）先探测网路上任务相关信息的组织方式，以指导后续节点扩展；4）在同构兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA四个基准上，WebSwarm在深度、广度和深度-广度交织任务中均一致优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: WebSwarm通过递归委托和证据驱动的自适应扩展，有效解决了复杂搜索任务中深度与覆盖率的平衡问题。消融实验、任务难度分析、网页工具效率和模型泛化性分析进一步验证了其有效性，并为多智能体搜索系统设计提供了有益洞见。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [114] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度研究系统中，将LLM用作引用质量评审的奖励模型时的校准问题。通过在对抗性长文本基准上对8个现成LLM评审员进行1,248项评审决策的评分（含人工审核和378个争议案例），发现较便宜的评审员在来源相关性和事实支持两个维度上具有竞争力，但标量F1分数掩盖了方向性偏差，校准评审员是将其用作奖励信号的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练中越来越依赖LLM评审员作为奖励模型，但在信任该信号之前，需要了解评审员的能力下限和偏差程度。特别是在深度研究系统中，引用质量评审涉及来源相关性和事实支持两个维度，评审员的校准问题直接关系到下游强化学习训练的质量。

Method: 构建对抗性长文本基准数据集，包含1,248项评审决策（全部经人工审核，其中378个为评审员分歧产生的疑难案例）。对来自3个模型家族的8个现成LLM评审员进行评分，与金标准标签对比。评估指标包括：F1分数、Cohen's κ一致性系数、通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率等方向性偏差指标。

Result: GPT-5-mini在来源相关性维度上达到最高的通过类F1分数0.908（κ=0.636）；在事实支持维度上，所有评审员在统计上无法区分（置信区间重叠），没有单一模型占优。但在可比F1水平下，评审员在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上存在显著差异，标量F1掩盖了这种方向性偏差。

Conclusion: 将引用评审标准用作奖励信号前，对评审员进行校准是必要前提。校准并不需要最昂贵的模型，较便宜的评审员即可胜任。方向性偏差（而非仅标量F1）是选择评审员的关键考量因素，因为下游强化学习会放大这些偏差。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [115] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以葡萄牙国家语言模型AMALIA为案例，提出"恢复差距"方法来检验模型编码道德基础构念时的效度问题。尽管AMALIA在表面一致率上与人类编码者接近，但当将整体提示分解为代码簿原子条款并按理论规则重组后，其性能大幅下降，表明模型依赖表面关联（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒）而非真正理解构念理论。研究者主张国家模型评估体系不仅应测量与人编码者的一致率，还需考察达成一致的证据路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型虽在标注任务上与人类编码者达到高一致率，但一致率仅反映信度而非效度。对于需要推理的构念（如道德基础），模型可能通过表面特征捷径而非真正遵循构念理论来达成正确的编码，这威胁到其作为测量工具的有效性。

Method: 研究者提出"恢复差距"方法：将整体提示分解为代码簿的原子条款，再按构念理论的显式规则重组。通过对比整体提示与分解-重组后的性能损失，评估模型是否真正遵循构念理论。将该方法应用于AMALIA-9B在权威道德基础的编码任务，并与经校准的英文工具及开放多语言LLM进行跨模型、跨语言对比。

Result: AMALIA在分解-重组后仅恢复约一半的整体性能，误差分析显示其依赖表面关联（尤其是权威人物附近的道德愤怒）。而开放多语言LLM在相同葡萄牙语语料和指令下能够弥合恢复差距，排除了语料本身为主要解释因素。

Conclusion: AMALIA可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚不足以作为独立测量构念的工具。本研究为单一反例而非对国家模型的全面否定。核心主张是：主权国家LLM的基准评测体系应同时检验与人类编码者的一致率及其达成一致的证据路径（即效度担保）。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [116] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是首个以能力驱动（capability-driven）的基准，用于评估在动态真实世界环境中运行的主动式智能体（proactive agents）。它围绕五项基础模型能力设计了400个双语任务，并在实时Docker容器中通过闭环多轮评估策略进行细粒度评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在三大缺陷：(1) 依赖沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，无法反映真实场景；(2) 基于场景的任务分类混合了多种模型能力，难以定位智能体失败的根本原因；(3) 静态预录答案无法适应动态环境。因此需要一个能力驱动、真实环境下的细粒度评估基准。

Method: 提出UniClawBench，核心设计包括：(1) 以五项基础能力（Skill Usage、Exploration、Long-Context Reasoning、Multimodal Understanding、Cross-Platform Coordination）为驱动，构建400个双语真实世界任务；(2) 在实时Docker容器中运行评估，采用逐步完成检查点（step-by-step completion checkpoints）进行细粒度评分；(3) 设计闭环评估策略，由执行者智能体、隐藏监督者智能体和用户智能体三方组成，模拟真实多轮人类反馈，同时防止评分标准泄露；(4) 在多种智能体框架下评估SOTA模型，解耦基础模型能力与框架设计的影响。

Result: 通过跨模型和跨框架的全面比较，揭示了基础模型能力与智能体框架设计如何共同塑造真实世界环境中的性能表现。（具体数值结果未在摘要中披露。）

Conclusion: UniClawBench 提供了一种更有效的主动式智能体评估范式：能力驱动的任务设计有助于精准诊断失败原因，闭环多轮评估更贴近真实交互场景。基准和代码已开源，以推动后续研究。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [The Regularization Parameter: Sparse Precision Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07735)
*Aryan Eftekhari,Daniel Sergio Vega,Ernst-Jan Camiel Wit,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于一阶最优性条件采样分布的闭式矩阵值正则化参数，用于高维稀疏精度矩阵估计，无需交叉验证即可实现与交叉验证相当的估计精度、更优的支持恢复，并将运行时间降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维低样本的稀疏精度矩阵估计中，正则化参数的选择至关重要，直接影响估计量的稀疏性和拟合平衡。传统方法依赖交叉验证，计算代价高昂且缺乏理论保证，因此迫切需要一种有理论依据、计算高效的自动化正则化参数选择方法。

Method: 作者从ℓ₁正则化高斯最大似然估计的一阶最优性条件出发，推导其重采样下的抽样分布，并基于该分布构造一个闭式、矩阵值的正则化参数。核心思想是预先指定在重采样下每个非零条目满足其最优性条件的概率，从而直接确定正则化参数，省去交叉验证步骤。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提正则化参数在标准条件下具备渐近标度性质，可保证估计量的一致性和稀疏一致性（sparsistency）。在合成高斯/非高斯数据、基因微阵列和神经影像真实应用中，该方法估计精度与交叉验证相当，支持恢复更优，运行时间减少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法为稀疏精度矩阵估计提供了一种理论上合理、计算上高效的正则化参数选择方案，在多种数据和实际应用场景中均展现出竞争力，有望替代传统交叉验证方法。

Abstract: Sparse precision matrix estimation provides an interpretable and computationally efficient framework for modeling conditional dependencies in high-dimensional, low-sample-size data. A recurring challenge is appropriately selecting the regularization parameter that controls estimator sparsity and strikes a balance between underfitting and overfitting. We propose a closed-form, matrix-valued regularization parameter derived from the sampling distribution of the first-order optimality conditions of the $\ell_1$-regularized Gaussian maximum-likelihood estimator. By prescribing the probability that each nonzero entry of the estimator satisfies its optimality condition under resampling, we eliminate the need for cross-validation. The resulting regularization parameter is shown to attain asymptotic scaling properties that, under standard conditions, provide consistency and sparsistency of the estimator. On synthetic Gaussian and non-Gaussian datasets, as well as real-world gene microarray and neuroimaging applications, the proposed approach achieves estimation accuracy comparable to cross-validation, delivers superior support recovery, and reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [118] [Distributionally Faithful Imputation via Positive Semi-Definite Kernel Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07767)
*Andrea Basteri,Carlo Ciliberto,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 PSD Impute，将 MCAR 下的缺失值插补重新定义为从掩码观测中进行密度估计，利用正半定核密度获得凸经验风险问题，通过牛顿内点法求解，兼具统计一致性与快速自适应超额风险界，并在多个数据集上展现出有竞争力的分布精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数插补方法依赖启发式或限制性参数假设，忽略了联合数据分布。作者希望为 MCAR 缺失提供一种基于密度估计的统一框架，既保证统计性质又具备实用性能。

Method: 将 MCAR 插补形式化为带掩码观测的密度估计问题；采用正半定（PSD）核密度构建凸经验风险目标，其边缘分布具有闭式解；使用牛顿内点法进行优化求解；从同一拟合密度中同时产生单一和多重插补。

Result: PSD Impute 具有统计一致性，其快速自适应超额风险界能克服维度灾难（针对高度正则的概率分布）。在一个人工数据集和十一个真实数据集上的初步实验显示，与流行插补基线相比，分布准确性具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PSD Impute 将缺失值插补建立在严谨的密度估计框架之上，兼具理论保证和实用前景，实验表明其分布精度与现有方法相比具备竞争优势。

Abstract: Missing values undermine statistical inference and machine learning pipelines, yet most imputation methods rely on heuristics or restrictive parametric assumptions that ignore the joint data distribution. We recast imputation under missing completely at random (MCAR) as density estimation from masked observations: estimate a distribution whose observed marginals exactly match those in the data. Leveraging positive semi definite (PSD) kernel densities we obtain a convex empirical risk problem with closed form marginals, solvable by a Newton interior point method. The resulting PSD Impute model yields both single and multiple imputations from the same fitted density, enjoys statistical consistency with fast adaptive excess risk beating the curse of dimensionality for very regular probabilities. Preliminary experiments on one synthetic and eleven real world datasets already indicate competitive distributional accuracy compared with popular imputation baselines, suggesting strong practical promise.

</details>


### [119] [Expressivity and Statistical Trade-offs in Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07967)
*Viet Vu,Renyuan Xu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文识别出漂移Lipschitz预算K是控制扩散策略表达性和统计行为的核心量：K越大，近似精度越高（值函数误差~1/K），但统计复杂度也越大；最优权衡下，通用神经网络漂移的有限样本性能间隙为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})，单侧耗散类为Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在实践中展现出强大的多模态、非高斯动作分布建模能力，但其表达性的数学根源尚不清晰，且如何根据有限数据充分挖掘这一表达性缺乏理论指导。本文旨在从理论上量化扩散策略的表达性及其统计代价，并给出实用的超参数选择原则。

Method: 以漂移函数的Lipschitz常数K为核心分析量，分别推导表达性上界（值函数逼近误差O(1/K)）和匹配的下界；将漂移参数化为神经网络，分析近似误差与统计复杂度的权衡，推导有限样本性能间隙的收敛速率；通过数值实验验证K与样本量之间的权衡关系。

Result: 理论上：K-Lipschitz漂移的扩散策略可逼近最优确定性策略，值函数误差为O(1/K)，且在非退化扩散噪声下有匹配下界。统计上：平衡近似与估计误差后，通用神经网络漂移的收敛速率为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})，单侧耗散类漂移可达更优速率Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})。实验证实了K的样本依赖性权衡。

Conclusion: 提出实用原则：根据可用样本量选择扩散预算K，再选取具有相应固定Lipschitz系数的神经网络架构，从而在有限数据下实现表达性与统计效率的最优平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as powerful policy parameterizations for reinforcement learning, representing state-conditioned action distributions as terminal laws of diffusion processes with parameterized drifts. This terminal-law representation has shown substantial expressive flexibility in practice, enabling diffusion policies to model complex, multimodal, and highly non-Gaussian action distributions; however, it remains unclear what mathematically drives this expressivity and how to fully exploit it when the policy is learned from finite data. In this paper, we identify the drift Lipschitz budget $K$ as a central quantity governing the expressivity and statistical behavior of diffusion policies. We quantify expressivity through approximation: diffusion policies with $K$-Lipschitz drifts can concentrate near optimal deterministic policies and achieve value approximation error of order $1/K$; moreover, we prove a matching lower bound under nondegenerate diffusion noise. This increased expressivity comes with a statistical cost. When the drift is parameterized by neural networks, increasing $K$ improves approximation but increases statistical complexity. Balancing these two terms yields a finite-sample performance gap of order $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$ for generic neural-network drifts, and a sharper rate $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$ for one-sided dissipative drift classes, where $n$ is the sample size and $m$ is the dimension of the state space. Numerical experiments provide empirical evidence for the sample-dependent trade-off in $K$, supporting both theoretical regimes. Our framework also suggests a practical implementation principle: choose the diffusion budget $K$ according to the available sample size, and then select a neural-network architecture with the corresponding fixed Lipschitz coefficient.

</details>


### [120] [Bayesian Experimental Design via Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08335)
*Angus Phillips,Gavin Kerrigan,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将贝叶斯实验设计中期望信息增益的双重不可解性问题与策略学习解耦：先独立求解一个得分匹配问题来近似EIG，再用该近似以单重不可解的方式训练策略网络，从而将原本的乘法成本转化为加法成本，大幅降低策略训练的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于策略的贝叶斯实验设计（BED）方法虽能学习自适应策略网络，但训练过程中期望信息增益（EIG）的双重不可解性导致需要昂贵或复杂的近似，严重限制了在策略优化本身上投入的计算资源，使得架构搜索、超参数调优或多策略选择变得极为困难。

Method: 核心思想是将EIG的双重不可解性与策略学习分离：第一步，求解一个与策略无关的得分匹配（score matching）问题，学习EIG的近似；第二步，利用学到的得分近似，以单重不可解的方式训练策略网络。这本质上将EIG估计与策略优化的乘法耦合成本转变为加法成本。

Result: 实验中成功训练了多个有竞争力的策略网络，且似然评估的成本并未随策略数量倍增；通过选择最优策略（即使不进行超参数或架构搜索）即可提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过得分匹配解耦EIG近似与策略学习，使得策略训练的计算成本从乘法变为加法，为BED策略的大规模训练、调优和选择开辟了可行的路径。

Abstract: Policy-based approaches to Bayesian experimental design (BED) allow the learning of deep policy networks that adaptively make intelligent design decisions based on previously collected data. However, the training of such policies is often held back by a fundamental challenge: the double intractability of the expected information gain (EIG). This necessitates expensive or complex approximations that restrict the effort one can invest in optimising the policy itself. To address this, we show that the double intractability of the EIG can be isolated from the policy learning by first solving a score matching problem that is independent of the policy used, then using the learned score approximation to train the policy in a singly intractable manner. This turns the key multiplicative cost into an additive one and reduces the computational burden on the policy training itself, making it far cheaper to train the policy multiple times when needed, e.g. for architecture search, hyperparameter tuning, or avoiding local optima. In our experiments we train multiple competitive policies without inducing a multiplicative cost in likelihood evaluations, which can increase performance by allowing us to select the best policy even without performing hyperparameter or architecture searches.

</details>


### [121] [Prediction-Powered Active Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08347)
*Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Valentin Kilian,Daolang Huang,Tom Rainforth,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出预测驱动的主动测试框架（PPAT），将无偏LURE估计量与基于预测的控制变量相结合，通过残差化损失函数降低方差，同时推导出面向方差缩减的最优采集策略，并建立了渐近正态性以提供有效置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动测试的估计器未能利用强大黑盒模型的预测信息，而这些预测在标签昂贵场景中日益可得。传统方法仅将预测作为有偏伪标签使用，无法兼顾无偏性与方差缩减。

Method: 提出PPAT框架：（1）将黑盒模型预测作为控制变量，对损失函数进行残差化处理，在保持无偏性的同时降低方差；（2）推导出面向PPAT方差最小化的oracle采集规则和实用的基于代理的采集规则；（3）证明PPAT的渐近正态性，构建渐近有效的置信区间。

Result: 在表格回归和图像分类任务上，PPAT在风险估计方面优于现有方法；其置信区间以显著更少的标签和更窄的区间宽度达到目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: PPAT通过将黑盒预测作为控制变量而非伪标签，实现了标签高效且统计性质优良的风险估计，为昂贵标注场景下的模型评估提供了一种实用且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: Active testing provides a label--efficient approach to risk estimation by adaptively selecting which test points should be labelled. However, existing estimators fail to exploit the informative predictions of powerful black--box models, even though such predictions are increasingly available in settings where labels remain expensive. To address this, we propose \textbf{Prediction--Powered Active Testing (PPAT)}, a novel label--efficient risk estimation framework that combines the unbiased LURE estimator \citep{farquhar2021statistical} with a prediction--powered control variate. Rather than using proxy predictions as biased pseudo--labels, PPAT uses them to residualise the loss, preserving unbiasedness while reducing variance. Beyond the estimator itself, PPAT also changes which points should be acquired: we derive oracle and practical surrogate--based acquisition rules tailored to reducing the variance of our estimator. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality for PPAT, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals and thus a principled estimate of the uncertainty around our estimates. Across tabular regression and image--classification tasks, PPAT outperforms existing methods in risk estimation, while its confidence intervals attain the target coverage with substantially fewer labels and smaller widths.

</details>


### [122] [Statistical Efficiency and Inference of Quantile Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08444)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计效率角度研究基于分位数的分布强化学习，聚焦分布策略评估。作者基于生成模型构造了分位数不动点估计量，建立了非渐近误差界（$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$），证明了$\sqrt{n}$最优参数收敛速率，刻画了渐近分布与半参数效率界，并进一步分析了分位数数量发散时的渐近效率，最后建立了Berry-Esseen定理以实现统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中，基于分位数的分布策略评估方法在统计效率方面的理论理解尚不充分。现有研究缺乏对分位数估计量的非渐近误差界、渐近分布、半参数效率界以及高维/无穷维极限下统计性质的系统刻画，这限制了统计推断和效率评估的理论基础。

Method: 考虑分位数投影的分布Bellman方程导出的分位数不动点$\eta_m$；假设有生成模型，基于经验MDP构造估计量$\eta_m^{(n)}$。在固定分位数$m$下，使用$W_\infty$度量建立非渐近误差界；推导$\sqrt{n}(\theta_m^{(n)}-\theta_m)$的渐近分布并刻画半参数效率界；分析$m$发散时的极限协方差结构并与非参数模型效率界匹配；对光滑泛函建立Berry-Esseen定理。

Result: （1）估计误差在$W_\infty$度量下为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$，达到最优参数$\sqrt{n}$收敛速率；（2）分位数参数渐近正态，且估计量达到半参数效率界；（3）$m$发散时极限协方差结构与非参数模型的半参数效率界匹配，分位数估计量在无穷维极限下保持渐近效率；（4）建立了光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理，为统计推断提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 基于分位数的分布策略评估具有样本高效性，估计量在固定维度和发散维度下均达到最优效率，且支持有效的统计推断。这为分布强化学习的理论分析和实际应用提供了坚实的统计基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning from the perspective of statistical efficiency. We focus on distributional policy evaluation, whose goal is to characterize the return distribution, namely the distribution of discounted cumulative rewards under a given policy. To obtain a finite-dimensional representation of the return distribution, we consider the quantile fixed point $η_m$ induced by the quantile-projected distributional Bellman equation. Assuming access to a generative model, we construct an estimator $η_m^{(n)}$ based on an empirical Markov decision process. For a fixed number of quantiles $m$, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound for $η_m^{(n)}$ and $η_m$ under the supremum $W_\infty$ metric, showing that the estimation error scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ with respect to $m$ and $n$. This implies that the quantile-based distributional policy evaluation problem can be solved with sample efficiency, achieving the optimal parametric $\sqrt{n}$ convergence rate. We derive the asymptotic distribution of the quantile parameters $\sqrt{n}(θ_m^{(n)}-θ_m)$ and characterize the semiparametric efficiency bound, which is attained by our estimator. Beyond the fixed-dimensional setting, we investigate the asymptotic regime in which the number of quantiles diverges. We characterize the limit covariance structure and show that it matches the semiparametric efficiency bound of the nonparametric model for distributional policy evaluation, showing that quantile-based estimators remain asymptotically efficient in the infinite-dimensional limit. Finally, we establish a Berry--Esseen theorem for smooth functionals $\sqrt{n}(η_m^{(n)}(s)-η_m(s))f$, thereby providing a foundation for statistically valid inference on functionals of the quantile-projected return distribution.

</details>


### [123] [High-Dimensional Procrustes Matching via Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08538)
*Xiaochun Niu,Tselil Schramm,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维Procrustes匹配问题，提出了一种基于"宽树"加权计数的多项式时间算法，可在常数相关性下实现精确恢复，并给出了改进的信息论下界和低阶优势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes匹配问题在低维情形(d=O(log n))已有充分研究，但高维情形(d≫log n)此前几乎无人触及。现有匹配保证即使仅要求信息论恢复，也需要近乎完美的相关性(ρ=1-o(1))。本文旨在填补这一空白，在常数相关性下实现高维精确恢复。

Method: 算法核心是计算并比较一组特殊选择的"宽树"(wide trees)的加权计数。具体而言，利用Otter树计数常数α≈0.338，设计多项式时间算法，当d≥polylog(n)且ρ²>√α时，可高概率成功恢复置换。

Result: (1) 算法结果：ρ²>√α时可在多项式时间内精确恢复；(2) 信息论保证：当ρ²≳max{log n/d, √(log n/n)}时精确恢复是可能的；(3) 低阶优势计算表明ρ²>√α是任何树计数算法的必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文将Procrustes匹配问题的研究拓展到高维情形，首次实现了常数相关性下的多项式时间精确恢复，并通过信息论下界和低阶分析刻画了算法的紧性，揭示了树计数方法在该问题中的根本局限。

Abstract: Suppose we observe two sets of $n$ Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, with the promise that, after applying a permutation of $[n]$ and a rotation of $\mathbb{R}^d$, the two sets are $ρ$-correlated. The Procrustes matching problem asks us to recover the unknown permutation of $[n]$ that aligns the two sets. The problem is well-studied in the low-dimensional regime $d=O(\log n)$, but the high-dimensional regime $d\gg \log n$ has remained largely uncharted: prior matching guarantees require nearly perfect correlation $ρ=1-o(1)$, even for information-theoretic recovery.
  Our main result is a polynomial-time algorithm for exact recovery at constant correlation. The algorithm works by computing and comparing weighted counts of a specially chosen family of ``wide'' trees. So long as $d\ge \mathrm{polylog}(n)$, the algorithm succeeds with high probability for any $ρ^2>\sqrtα$, where $α\approx 0.338$ is Otter's tree-counting constant.
  We complement this algorithmic result with an improved information-theoretic guarantee, showing that exact recovery is possible when $ρ^2 \gtrsim \max\{\log n/d,\sqrt{\log n/n}\}$. We also carry out a low-degree advantage calculation, which suggests that the condition $ρ^2 > \sqrtα$ is necessary for any tree-counting algorithm.

</details>


### [124] [Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08757)
*Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了扩散模型采样中的一个关键问题：即使得分函数的正向边际误差可以任意小，其离散化逆向采样器（如Euler–Maruyama）仍可能出现矩发散，导致Wasserstein距离发散，同时通过去噪器投影提供了一种简单有效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型理论通常依赖正向边际误差（forward-marginal error）来保证逆向采样的收敛性。然而，实际采样使用的是离散化的逆向时间过程，其轨迹可能偏离正向边际路径。作者旨在揭示这一理论与实践的鸿沟：小的正向边际误差并不能保证离散采样的数值稳定性和矩的收敛性。

Method: 作者分别从光滑得分场和固定有限神经网络架构两个角度构造反例：（1）构造一个光滑得分场，使其正向边际L²误差任意小，且精确逆向过程在路径空间全变差意义下任意接近，但其Euler–Maruyama离散化在概率收敛的同时所有正阶矩发散；（2）在固定神经网络架构内，构造一族有界、全局Lipschitz的去噪器，使得正向边际误差和路径空间全变差均趋于零，但离散采样端点在所有W_p下发散。正面结果：对于紧支撑数据，将学习的去噪器投影到包含支撑集的已知有界闭凸集上，可保持逐点精度，获得网格均匀矩界，并在温和局部正则性下得到Wasserstein收敛。实验使用小型固定DiT网络验证。

Result: 反例表明：Euler–Maruyama离散化可以在概率意义下弱收敛，但所有p≥1的Wasserstein距离W_p均发散。正面结果表明去噪器投影可抑制罕见数值轨迹上的大幅增长，同时保持整体轨迹误差较小。实验观察到未经投影的DiT网络在稀有轨迹上出现大幅增长，而去噪器投影有效抑制了这一现象。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的理论分析不能仅依赖正向边际误差；离散逆向采样的数值稳定性需要额外的结构性条件。去噪器投影是一种简单实用的正则化手段，可在不损害精度的前提下提供矩界和Wasserstein收敛保证。

Abstract: Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges.
  The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemExplainer，一种面向时序图神经网络(TGN)的事后解释方法。通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树，结合层级相关性传播(LRP)，追溯历史事件对模型预测的贡献，并设计优化目标替代top-k选择以提升忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TGN解释方法忽略了记忆模块（记录和更新节点历史的核心组件），无法揭示历史事件如何影响模型预测，导致解释不完整、不忠实。

Method: 提出两类树结构：(1)拓扑归因树，捕获邻居节点及其记忆向量的影响；(2)记忆回溯树，量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量。将LRP应用于TGN，确保事件总贡献等于模型logits。针对logits到概率的非线性映射导致top-k选择不忠实的问题，设计优化目标替代top-k，直接识别重要事件。

Result: 在9个时序图数据集上（涵盖节点属性预测、链接预测和图分类任务）进行实验，方法提供了忠实的解释，且性能优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: MemExplainer通过联合建模拓扑影响与记忆回溯，首次将LRP引入TGN解释，解决了记忆模块被忽视的核心问题，显著提升了TGN预测解释的忠实性和全面性。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [126] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究胸部X光片(CXR)分类中长尾多标签模型从分数转换为决策后的公平性问题，通过在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上的审计，发现结合组尾加权和尾感知阈值选择可显著降低罕见阳性患者的漏诊率，但残存漏诊率仍较高，结论是罕见标签公平性依赖于发现类型、子群和操作阈值的联合作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使在CXR分类中达到可接受的排序性能，罕见阳性患者仍可能被决策阈值排除在外，尤其是在特定子群中。现有研究多关注排序指标，缺乏对"分数转换为决策后谁被漏诊"这一部署前公平性问题的系统审计。

Method: 作者提出"诊断阶梯"(diagnostic ladder)分析框架，逐层分离四个因素：(1)类别级长尾损失、(2)子群感知加权、(3)组鲁棒性(GroupDRO)、(4)阈值选择。在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT两个数据集上，使用配对bootstrap对比检验阈值化后的假阴性率(FNR)降低是否显著，并以GroupDRO作为组鲁棒性的参考基线。

Result: 在VinDr-CXR上，组尾加权加尾感知阈值将尾部FNR从0.665降至0.269，性别最差组FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差组FNR从0.822降至0.133，同时宏观mAP从0.611提升至0.635。在MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT上，同样策略将尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，并降低了跨性别、年龄、种族和保险的最差组FNR，但残存漏诊率仍然较高。配对bootstrap支持FNR降低的显著性，GroupDRO参考运行表明单独的组鲁棒性无法消除罕见子群漏诊。

Conclusion: 研究支持一个窄审计主张：CXR中罕见标签公平性并非仅取决于标签频率或排序指标，而是由发现类型(finding)、子群(subgroup)和操作阈值(operating threshold)三者共同决定。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [127] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Transformer类神经常微分算子中标准注意力机制的二次复杂度与缺乏局部偏置两大问题，本文提出局部线性Transformer（LLT），结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并引入坐标与几何信息，在多种PDE问题上实现了竞争性的精度与显著的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力在PDE算子学习中有两大局限：(1) 计算复杂度随网格节点数二次增长，难以扩展到大规模问题；(2) 缺乏对局部相互作用的显式偏置，而PDE解本质上具有局部依赖特性。因此需要一种既能捕捉长程依赖又能高效处理局部结构的架构。

Method: 提出Local Linear Transformer (LLT)：将线性化全局注意力（降低复杂度至线性）与局部空间混合模块相结合，同时显式嵌入坐标和几何信息，以兼顾全局依赖与局部交互。该方法适用于结构化与非结构化网格、多种离散化方式（有限元、有限体积、有限差分）。

Result: 在弹性、塑性、翼型绕流、管道流、Darcy流等多个PDE问题上，LLT相比其他神经常微分算子和Transformer基线取得了竞争性或更低的相对L2误差。在匹配的结构化离散下，单次训练迭代的墙钟时间比Transolver减少1.8至2.5倍。此外，成功扩展到三维汽车空气动力学数据集（每样本32,186个非结构化网格点）。

Conclusion: LLT在不同离散化方式、网格类型和问题设置下均能提供精确且计算高效的PDE算子学习方案，在精度-效率权衡上表现优异，具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [128] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA 提出一种频谱感知的持续微调框架，通过对当前有效权重进行递归奇异值分解，将其拆分为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和新适配器，从而在任务序列中避免灾难性遗忘，在六任务 GLUE 基准上以更少参数取得最优平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LoRA 类方法在持续微调场景下，每个新任务的低秩适配器堆叠在同一冻结权重上，导致新任务覆盖旧任务知识（灾难性遗忘）。需要一种能够吸收先前任务知识、避免覆盖的持续微调方法。

Method: ReCoLoRA 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从预训练权重的随机化 SVD 初始化适配器；(2) 通过肘部准则选择每层有效秩；(3) 先更新主子空间再开放残差容量。关键创新在于「递归整合」：每个新任务开始前，对当前有效权重（而非原始权重）重新进行 SVD 分解，将其拆分为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和全新适配器，使新任务从已吸收前序知识的模型出发。

Result: 在四个 7-8B 规模骨干模型上执行六任务持续 GLUE 序列，ReCoLoRA 在三个骨干模型上取得最佳最终平均得分，对比基线包括 rank-swept LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA 和 DoRA，且训练参数更少。此外，oracle-routed 任务库变体作为完全任务隔离的上界。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA 通过频谱感知的递归低秩分解，有效解决了持续微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，在性能和参数效率上均优于现有 LoRA 变体，为 LLM 的终身学习提供了一种简洁且高效的方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [129] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omni-Sleep 是一个睡眠基础模型，利用 CNS/ANS 生理分区作为拓扑约束先验，通过系统内一致性、系统间同步和潜在空间掩码时序建模三个目标学习结构化表征，在超过10万小时多中心PSG数据上预训练，在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型在融合多模态生理信号（EEG、EOG、EMG、ECG、呼吸等）时通常忽略其生理组织拓扑结构，以拓扑不可知的方式简单融合，未能利用中枢神经系统（CNS）与自主神经系统（ANS）之间的层级化生理组织关系。

Method: Omni-Sleep 以 CNS/ANS 分区作为生理先验，通过三个学习目标实现拓扑约束的表征学习：(1) 系统内一致性——捕捉神经信号和心肺信号内部共享的子系统层面因素；(2) 系统间同步——对齐子系统轨迹以建模脑-体动态；(3) 潜在空间掩码时序建模——捕捉长时程睡眠动态。模型在超过100,000小时的多中心多模态PSG数据上预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上，Omni-Sleep 在多个数据集和不同模态消融设置下均优于现有强基础模型基线，展现出更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力以及对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将生理层级结构（CNS/ANS 分区）作为先验纳入表征学习，能显著提升睡眠表征的泛化能力，为睡眠分析基础模型的设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [130] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息价值路由的块稀疏注意力机制，通过查询级自适应预算分配解决传统top-k选择的"近视"问题，在多个模型和基准上显著提升召回率，接近密集注意力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统块稀疏注意力（如SSA风格）对每个查询固定选取top-k个关键块，但当第k和第k+1块得分接近时，会因预算不足而丢弃可能包含关键证据的块，导致不可恢复的信息丢失。这种"近视"截断成为长上下文模型的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种信息价值路由器（Value-of-Information Router），对每个查询评估top-k截断的"决断度"（即第k与第k+1块得分差距），对差距最小的查询将其保留块数翻倍。该方法与骨干架构无关，可与Quest等现有块评分方法叠加使用，并实现融合选择与内核的流水线。

Result: 在LongBench-v2 medium（n=215）上，路由器叠加Quest的配对召回率达0.75（对比top-k的0.47），提升28个百分点（McNemar p<0.01）；在RULER NIAH multikey上与密集注意力精度差距在2个百分点以内。在Qwen2.5、Mistral-Nemo、Qwen3.6等四种模型三种架构上均复现提升。128K上下文下，Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3.6分别保留密集精度的0.81和0.89（对比SSA风格top-k的0.09），推理速度分别为密集的0.62x和0.80x wall time。

Conclusion: 通过查询级自适应预算分配的信息价值路由，有效缓解了块稀疏注意力的近视截断问题，在保持推理加速的同时大幅缩小与密集注意力的精度差距，展现出良好的跨模型和跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [131] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT是一个基于Transformer的生存预测模型，能够直接处理不完整的基因组输入，无需测试时插补，通过掩码自注意力和特征可用性掩码实现对异构缺失模式的鲁棒性，在多中心胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞癌数据上展现了强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组预测模型跨机构部署时，由于不同站点使用不同的测序panel，导致结构化的特征缺失。现有方法（限制共享基因、排除不完整患者、测试时插补）均存在缺陷，会降低鲁棒性并限制多中心数据的利用。因此需要一种能够直接处理不完整基因组特征的生存预测方法。

Method: 提出SHIFT模型：1）对每个基因组特征进行独立表示；2）使用掩码自注意力机制（masked self-attention），结合特征可用性掩码，使预测仅基于观察到的输入；3）训练期间引入可变比率特征掩码（variable-rate feature masking）以增强对异构缺失模式的鲁棒性；4）单一模型即可适应不同的特征集合。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞状细胞癌的多队列外部验证中（包括严重的跨队列panel不匹配场景），SHIFT展现出强泛化能力，优于标准生存分析基线和基于插补的方法。此外，在模型开发阶段纳入不完整队列的患者可提升外部数据上的性能。

Conclusion: 缺失感知建模是多中心精准肿瘤学生存预测的一种实用策略，部分观测的队列不应被排除在模型构建之外，SHIFT为跨机构的基因组预后建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [132] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LiST（Lipschitz Scaling Training），一种通过迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数实现神经网络开箱即用校准的训练范式，揭示了Lipschitz约束与温度缩放之间的理论联系，并以校准作为选择精度-鲁棒性Pareto前沿工作点的原则性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络通常难以同时满足精度、鲁棒性和校准性三大指标。Lipschitz约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但其约束常数的选择依赖人工调参，且校准特性鲜有研究。作者旨在以校准为准则，自动确定Lipschitz常数，从而在精度-鲁棒性权衡中找到最优工作点。

Method: 首先，作者理论证明并实验验证了Lipschitz常数L与温度缩放之间存在对应关系，即存在某个L*使网络天然校准。基于此提出LiST：训练中迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数以收敛至该校准工作点；通过损失函数中的margin参数，可构建全程校准的Pareto前沿；最终在收敛阶段将校准数据重新整合进训练，提升样本效率且不牺牲校准性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上验证，LiST在精度和鲁棒性方面与受约束及无约束基线方法相比具有竞争力，同时保持开箱即用的校准性。

Conclusion: 校准可作为选择Lipschitz常数的有效准则，LiST成功实现了精度、鲁棒性与校准性的三合一，并提供了可在校准状态下自由导航精度-鲁棒性权衡的实用方案。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [133] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jet-Long 是一种无训练的零样本上下文扩展方法，通过双焦点注意力机制（局部RoPE忠实窗口+动态缩放长程窗口）与包含-排除注意力融合及即时RoPE校正旋转，在短上下文保持模型原始精度，在长上下文实现稳定外推，性能超越现有基线，且推理开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在检索增强生成、仓库级编码和智能体工作流等场景中，输入长度常远超预训练窗口。现有零样本上下文扩展方法通常预先固定单一缩放因子：激进因子牺牲短上下文保真度，保守因子在长上下文时失效。因此需要一种能在短输入时精确恢复基座模型、长输入时平滑外推的自适应方法。

Method: Jet-Long 采用双焦点（bifocal）架构：(1) 局部窗口保持RoPE原始频率，确保短上下文忠实度；(2) 长程窗口的缩放因子随序列长度动态调整；(3) 使用包含-排除（inclusion-exclusion）注意力合并机制融合两个窗口的输出；(4) 在线RoPE校正旋转（on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation）补偿相位差异。所有操作融合进单个 CuTe 内核，实现几乎零推理开销。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 上测试，128K 上下文下 Jet-Long 在 RULER 指标上分别超出最强基线 +4.79/+2.18/+2.03 个百分点；在 HELMET-RAG 基准上取得最佳整体准确率；在 PG-19 上获得最低困惑度。长上下文预填充在 H100 上达到 FA2 的 1.39 倍吞吐量，单批生成在各长度下开销 ≤ 4%。方法可泛化至 Jet-Nemotron 等混合注意力架构，且对超参数不敏感。

Conclusion: Jet-Long 作为一种无训练、零样本的双焦点上下文扩展方法，成功解决了短上下文保真与长上下文外推之间的权衡困境，在多个模型规模和基准上取得最优性能，同时兼具推理高效性和部署易用性，并展现出良好的架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [134] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bubeck、Li和Nagaraj关于两层神经网络Lipschitz常数的猜想：对于一般数据，任何拟合n个噪声标签的m神经元两层网络，其Lipschitz常数至少约为√(n/m)量级，证明对任意连续分段线性激活函数（含ReLU）成立，仅差一个对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bubeck、Li和Nagaraj猜想：在无权重大小限制的条件下，拟合n个噪声标签的两层网络必然具有至少√(n/m)量级的Lipschitz常数。Bubeck和Sellke在参数多项式有界条件下证明了普适版本，但两层无界权重的情形需要全新方法。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 核心创新是用函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖（后者对无界权重不可行）。中心技术工具是一个"刚性引理"：在B₂和d≥3的单位球面S^{d-1}上，每个规范折点的系数由实现函数的Lipschitz常数控制，因为不同超平面上的折点在一般点处无法相互抵消。证明还建立了"实现折点计数"版本，用实际折点超平面数k(f)+1替代参数数目m̄。

Result: 对d≥3球面均匀分布或N(0, I_d/d)高斯数据、噪声水平σ²>0的标签，任意两层网络（含任意实权重、偏置和仿射跳跃连接），若拟合误差低于噪声底限ε，则以高概率有Lip(f) ≥ c·ε·√(n/(m̄ log(C m̄ n d/ε)))，其中m̄=(K-1)m+1。实现折点版本以k(f)+1替代m̄。d=2时刚性失效，并构造了宽度2n、Lipschitz常数O(1)的两层ReLU插值器作为匹配下界。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了无界权重两层网络泛化所需的Lipschitz常数下界，揭示了过参数化并不能规避Lipschitz代价。d=2的刚性失效及其匹配构造说明维度对结论至关重要，深化了对神经网络表示能力与光滑性之间权衡的理解。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [135] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaNCA 是一个元学习框架，通过神经细胞自动机的局部规则来生成人工神经网络的权重，无需反向传播即可产生多种架构的任务网络，并在未见过的架构上展现出泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经元通过局部突触交互实现高效学习和终身适应的启发，作者旨在利用神经细胞自动机（NCA）仅依赖局部更新规则实现形态发生的成功经验，将其扩展到人工神经网络权重的自组织生成上，从而实现无需反向传播、具有适应性和鲁棒性的权重生成方法。

Method: 提出 Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA) 框架：一个可学习的规则网络通过局部交互在计算图上迭代更新任务网络的权重。规则网络采用新设计的 Weight Transformer 架构，利用线性注意力机制聚合来自相邻权重和隐藏状态的信号。元训练后，规则网络可为不同架构的任务网络生成权重，全程无需反向传播。

Result: MetaNCA 成功为 MNIST 和 CIFAR-100 上的前馈 MLP、CNN 和 ResNet 生成权重，可扩展至 200 万参数规模的网络。此外，MetaNCA 能泛化到元训练期间未见过的架构，且训练阶段中的架构多样性能够进一步增强这种泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaNCA 展示了通过局部规则自组织生成神经网络权重的可行性，为摆脱反向传播的权重生成范式提供了新思路，其泛化到未知架构的能力暗示了该方法在神经架构搜索和持续学习等领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [136] [Reinforcing the Generation Order of Multimodal Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08056)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhe Wang,Sourav Bhabesh,Dmitriy Bespalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可学习控制模块的自适应生成顺序优化方法，通过GRPO训练该模块，同时提升扩散语言模型在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明自适应token生成顺序可提升数学推理和代码合成性能，但作者发现仅靠模型logits无法为文本到图像生成和多模态理解确定最优生成序列，需要引入可学习的顺序控制机制。

Method: 引入一个可学习的控制模块（learnable control module），采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行训练，用于动态决定扩散语言模型的生成顺序。

Result: 在GenEval文本到图像对齐基准上取得4.08%的相对提升；在VLMEvalKit多模态理解基准上取得4.85%的相对提升，同时在细粒度空间关系捕捉和多模态推理任务上也表现出显著增强。

Conclusion: 学习生成顺序控制可有效提升扩散语言模型在文本到图像对齐和多模态理解上的表现，验证了该方法在不同模态任务上的广泛有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in natural language generation tasks. Recent research demonstrates that adaptive token generation ordering can significantly improve performance in mathematical reasoning and code synthesis applications. In this work, we investigate the optimization of generation order for both text-to-image synthesis and multimodal understanding. We first establish that, unlike structured problems in language generation such as Sudoku puzzles, model logits alone are insufficient for determining optimal generation sequences in text-to-image generation and multimodal understanding. To address this challenge, we introduce a learnable control module trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to determine the generation order. Our results demonstrate that learning this control block substantially improves both text-to-image alignment and multimodal understanding in DLMs. In particular, it enhances the model's ability to capture fine-grained spatial relationships in generated images while also strengthening performance on multimodal reasoning and comprehension tasks. We evaluate our framework on GenEval, an object-focused benchmark for text-to-image alignment, where it achieves 4.08% relative improvements. In addition, experiments on VLMEvalKit confirm 4.85% relative improvements in multimodal understanding, highlighting the broad effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [137] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段流水线方法，通过解耦语法习得与算法推理，解决了小语言模型在低资源编程语言上代码生成性能差的问题。该方法先用离线数据合成引擎生成验证过的训练数据，再进行监督微调，最后结合执行验证奖励的强化学习。在 Julia 和 Ballerina 上取得了显著提升，且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Python等主流语言上代码生成表现优异，但在Julia等低资源编程语言上性能急剧下降。提升小语言模型在这些语言上的能力面临"三难困境"：监督微调受限于数据稀缺，推理时扩展部署成本过高，从头开始强化学习几乎无收益。本文旨在破解这一困境。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：第一阶段，将推理时计算"左移"到离线数据合成引擎，利用迭代编译器反馈和测试反馈生成经过验证的训练样本；第二阶段，在合成验证数据上对小语言模型进行监督微调，嵌入强语法先验；第三阶段，应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），以语言无关的输入/输出测试作为奖励信号，SFT先验约束探索过程避免语法错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，Julia的MultiPL-E pass@1提升最高+7.6点，Agnostics LiveCodeBench提升+14.2点，且仅使用了先前SOTA方法1/3的数据和1/6的成本。在Ballerina上达到49.7% MultiPL-E Pass@1，该语言几乎无预训练表征。消融实验证实SFT阶段和执行验证奖励对稳定训练均必不可少。

Conclusion: 通过将语法习得与算法推理解耦，三阶段流水线有效破解了小语言模型在低资源编程语言上的三难困境，实现了性能提升与成本降低的双重收益，并展现出对极低资源语言的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [138] [A Transdiagnostic Space of Disorder Like Phenotypes in Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07753)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将心理障碍建模为可在强化学习智能体中通过剂量可控的认知评估信号旋钮进行操控的框架，在评估引导的PPO智能体中实现七种障碍，每种障碍均表现出分级、单调的剂量-反应曲线，并揭示了障碍的自组织情感空间、缓解机制的非对称性以及共病非线性交互等涌现发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在RL智能体中仅诱导一两种障碍，依赖手工调参的奖励塑形、事后标注行为且仅报告单次运行。本研究旨在建立一个系统化、可复现、剂量可控的心理障碍建模框架，为计算精神病学提供严格测试平台，并探索情感控制的失效模式。

Method: 在评估引导的PPO（Appraisal-Guided PPO）智能体中，将七种障碍（焦虑、躁狂、强迫检查、抑郁、冲动、成瘾、创伤后应激）分别建模为单一的认知评估信号旋钮，每个旋钮基于计算精神病学理论，每个症状通过预先注册的、映射到公认范式的检测方法测量。超过一千次运行（10种子、4对照、95%置信区间）进行验证。还测试了旋钮移除的缓解效果、分级暴露课程以及双旋钮交互，并在MiniWorld像素环境中验证了三个障碍旋钮的可迁移性。

Result: 每种障碍均表现出分级、单调的剂量-反应曲线，所有对照均无法重现。三个涌现发现：(1)障碍在两维情感空间中自组织，躁狂与焦虑呈镜像关系；(2)移除旋钮可缓解奖励扭曲型障碍（躁狂、强迫检查、成瘾），但回避型障碍（焦虑、PTSD）需通过分级暴露课程恢复；(3)双旋钮同时操作产生非加性交互，产生可检验的共病预测。三个障碍旋钮在MiniWorld像素环境中成功迁移，且跨领域检测分离得到确认。

Conclusion: 评估权重参数化了一个可控的情感表型空间，同一旋钮既能诱导障碍也能模拟其治疗。该框架不局限于网格世界或PPO的评估批评家，具有良好的泛化性，为计算精神病学提供了系统化的建模与治疗探索平台。

Abstract: Modelling psychological disorders in artificial agents offers both a testbed for computational psychiatry and a lens on the failure modes of affective control. Prior work induces one or two disorders in a reinforcement learning (RL) agent by hand-tuned reward shaping, labels the behaviour post hoc, and reports single runs.
