Without accounting for such patient-level heterogeneity, classical statistical inference frameworks for combination therapy might be inadequate and overly optimistic. In this paper, we introduce a novel and model-free statistical inference framework to complement the classical one and evaluate combination therapy after adjusting for patient-level heterogeneity in responses to monotherapies. We address the non-identifiability and nonlinearity issue inherent in adjustment of patient-level heterogeneity and establish conditions for the (partial) identifiability of the cross-world target parameter. We develop an outcome-based optimal matching scheme to achieve asymptotic normality and construct $\sqrt{N}$-rate confidence intervals for the target parameter, thereby enabling reliable, efficient and transparent evaluation of combination therapy amid patient-level heterogeneity. The benefits of the proposed framework are demonstrated through a reanalysis of the ACTG 175 trial.

</details>


### [278] [Bias-Corrected Multiplier Bootstrap Inference for Spectral Edges of Large Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08089)
*Xiucai Ding,Yichen Hu,Jiahui Xie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断问题，本文提出一种偏差校正的乘数自助法，通过精心设计的乘数扰动将边缘波动正则化至略大于Tracy-Widom的尺度，使得高斯近似可行。该方法能构造有效的置信区间，并可作为无阈值的spike数量估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断的核心困难在于：最大的非spike样本特征值在Tracy-Widom尺度下波动，而有效的统计推断需要精确的中心化（确定性谱边缘）和尺度常数，这两者在一般未知总体协方差结构下难以估计。现有方法在实践中面临估计精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出偏差校正的乘数自助法(bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap)。核心思路：引入精心校准的乘数扰动，将边缘波动正则化至略大于Tracy-Widom的尺度，在该尺度下高斯近似变得可行。具体通过自助特征值构造置信区间，并引入数据驱动的再中心化步骤来校正自助法引起的确定性边缘偏移。理论上证明，经偏差校正和重缩放后，最大的几个非spike自助特征值在给定数据的条件下渐近服从高斯分布。

Result: （1）所提置信区间渐近有效，区间长度仅略大于Tracy-Widom尺度；（2）在备择假设下（额外spike以大于n^{-1/6}的局部尺度从bulk中分离），覆盖率趋于零；（3）同一置信区间可产生一个无阈值的spike数量估计器，不要求spike互异或非常大；（4）该方法为碎石图(scree plot)提供了数据驱动且理论上有据的截断点。

Conclusion: 所提出的偏差校正乘数自助法为高维协方差矩阵的谱边缘推断提供了一种实用且理论上严格的解决方案，同时具有spike数量估计和碎石图截断选择等附加应用价值。

Abstract: Inference for spectral edges of large covariance matrices is a fundamental problem in high-dimensional statistics. A major difficulty is that the largest non-spiked sample eigenvalues, which serve as natural estimators of the edge, fluctuate on the Tracy--Widom scale. Consequently, valid inference requires accurate centering by the deterministic spectral edge together with a precise scaling constant, both of which are often difficult to estimate in practice under general unknown population covariance structures. In this paper, we propose a bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap procedure for inference on the deterministic edge of the bulk spectrum. The key idea is to introduce a carefully calibrated multiplier perturbation that regularizes the edge fluctuation to a slightly larger scale at which Gaussian approximation becomes tractable. The resulting confidence interval is constructed directly from bootstrap eigenvalues, together with a data-driven recentering step that corrects the bootstrap-induced shift of the deterministic edge. On the theoretical side, we show that, after bias correction and rescaling, the largest few non-spiked bootstrap eigenvalues are asymptotically Gaussian conditionally on the data. Building on this result, we establish the asymptotic validity of the proposed confidence interval, whose length is only slightly larger than the Tracy--Widom scale, and prove vanishing coverage under alternatives in which additional spikes separate from the bulk at a local scale larger than $n^{-1/6}$. As a consequence, the same confidence interval yields a threshold-free estimator for the number of spikes, without requiring the spikes to be distinct or very large. Equivalently, the procedure yields a data-driven and theoretically justified cutoff for the scree plot.

</details>


### [279] [Joint estimation of high-dimensional spiked covariance matrices via a partially shared subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08123)
*Changwon Yoon,Minwoo Kim,Sungkyu Jung,Jeongyoun Ahn*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种尖峰协方差模型，用于两个数据集部分共享协方差结构（共享一个子空间但各自保留独有方向）的场景，并给出包含子空间及秩估计、闭式池化权重的完整估计框架，附带渐近保证，应用于投资组合构建和脑肿瘤基因表达对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据分析常受限于小样本，而来自相关来源的辅助数据集往往可用。当两个数据集部分（而非完全）共享协方差结构时，利用共享部分可显著改善估计。现有模型无法灵活捕捉这种部分共享的场景。

Method: 提出对称处理两个数据集的尖峰协方差模型（spiked covariance model），其中共享一个未知秩、任意谱位置的子空间，同时各自保留独有尖峰方向。基于随机矩阵理论，在比例增长渐近机制下，开发了联合估计共享子空间及其秩的完整流程，并推导出组合两个数据集的闭式池化权重。该框架也填补了对比降维在高维设置中的估计器空白。

Result: 在比例增长渐近机制下提供了理论保证；通过COVID-19初期投资组合构建和脑肿瘤基因表达对比分析两个实证案例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型严格推广了现有共享协方差结构模型，对称处理两个数据集，提供了一套完整且有理论保证的估计方法，并解决了对比降维在高维场景下的估计器缺失问题。

Abstract: Statistical analysis of high-dimensional data is often hampered by limited sample sizes, yet auxiliary datasets from related sources are often readily available. When two such datasets share part of their covariance structure, but not all of it, exploiting the shared part can substantially improve estimation. We propose a spiked covariance model that explicitly captures this partial sharing: two datasets share a subspace of unknown rank and arbitrary position in the spectrum, while each retains its own distinct spiked directions. The model treats the two datasets symmetrically and strictly generalizes existing models for shared covariance structure. We develop a complete estimation procedure that includes joint estimation of the shared subspace and its rank, a closed-form pooling weight for combining the two datasets, and asymptotic guarantees derived from random matrix theory in the proportional-growth regime. The framework also resolves a gap in contrastive dimension reduction by providing a principled estimator for high-dimensional settings. We illustrate the methodology on portfolio construction during the early COVID-19 pandemic and on contrastive analysis of brain tumor gene expression.

</details>


### [280] [Bayesian Donor Set Selection in Synthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08142)
*Seul Lee,Johan Lim,Joungyoun Kim,Xinlei Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯层次模型，在合成控制法框架下同时进行供体集合选择与权重估计。通过层级Gamma-Bernoulli先验构造，模型可为不相关供体赋予精确零权重，从而自动筛选有效供体。论文证明了简化模型下的后验收敛性，并通过模拟和西德GDP案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成控制法(SCM)的效果高度依赖于供体集合的合理选择，但现有方法通常假设供体集合是预先固定的，缺乏在估计合成权重的同时自动筛选供体的机制。这在实际应用中存在局限性，尤其当供体池中包含不相关或弱相关单元时。

Method: 提出贝叶斯层次模型：(1) 采用层级Gamma-Bernoulli先验构造供体权重，使权重后验分布可集中于单纯形的面上，允许精确的零权重出现；(2) 实现合成控制权重与活跃供体集合的联合估计；(3) 在简化干预前模型下建立了供体集合的后验收敛性理论结果。

Result: 数值模拟表明：当供体池包含不相关或弱相关单元时，该模型在供体恢复和权重估计方面均优于传统方法；在全部供体均有效的情境下，模型仍保持竞争力。对西德GDP轨迹的实际应用进一步验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 将供体集合选择纳入贝叶斯合成控制框架，为现有方法提供了一种更简约且灵活的扩展方案。

Abstract: The Synthetic Control Method (SCM) is a widely used approach for assessing the effects of interventions by constructing a synthetic counterfactual using a donor set of untreated units. However, the effectiveness of SCM heavily relies on the careful selection of an appropriate donor set. In this paper, we propose a Bayesian hierarchical model that performs donor set selection while preserving the standard SCM simplex constraint on donor weights. Unlike approaches that assume a fixed donor set, our model allows for the simultaneous estimation of the synthetic control weights and the active donor set. By using a hierarchical Gamma-Bernoulli construction for the donor weights, the proposed model assigns posterior mass to simplex faces and allows exact zero weights for excluded donors. We establish a posterior donor-set consistency result under a simplified pre-intervention model. Through numerical simulations, we show that our model improves donor recovery and weight estimation when the donor pool contains irrelevant or weakly related units, while remaining competitive in full-donor settings. Finally, we apply our model to the GDP trajectory of West Germany, illustrating its practical applicability. Our findings suggest that incorporating donor set selection offers a more parsimonious and flexible extension of existing Bayesian synthetic control methods.

</details>


### [281] [Circular Expectiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08306)
*Bernhard Klar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文引入圆形expectile，作为基于弦距离的非对称圆形损失函数的极小化子。该优化问题非凸，需单独分析存在性与唯一性。文章将线性expectile扩展到方向数据，保留了圆形均值作为α=1/2时的对称特例，并证明了正密度分布下极小化子的存在唯一性，同时给出了经验版本的一致性及渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性expectile的概念推广到方向（圆形）数据领域。线性expectile在统计中已有广泛应用（如风险度量、分位数回归的替代），但方向数据的非对称分布刻画工具相对匮乏，需要一种能够处理圆形数据非对称性的新方法。

Method: 基于弦距离（chord distance）构造非对称圆形损失函数，定义圆形expectile为其极小化子。以样本圆形均值作为参考方向，建立圆上的线性序，从而定义经验圆形expectile。通过分析目标函数和识别函数的性质，给出几何解释，并利用分布的正密度条件证明解的存在性与唯一性，进一步推导经验版本的一致性和有限维渐近正态性。

Result: 在分布具有正密度的条件下，证明了圆形expectile的存在性和唯一性；经验圆形expectile同样具有唯一性，且满足一致性及有限维渐近正态分布。

Conclusion: 圆形expectile成功将线性expectile框架推广到方向数据，为圆形数据的离散度、偏度和对称性诊断提供了新的分析工具，具有潜在的应用前景。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce circular expectiles as minimizers of an asymmetric circular loss function based on chord distance. In contrast to the linear expectile criterion, the resulting circular optimization problem is non-convex, so existence and uniqueness require a separate analysis. The construction extends linear expectiles to directional data while preserving the circular mean as the symmetric case corresponding to $α=1/2$. We derive basic representations of the objective function and the associated identification function, and give a geometric interpretation that generalizes the corresponding representation for the circular mean. Furthermore, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the minimizers for distributions with positive density on the circle. The empirical circular expectile is defined by using the sample circular mean as reference direction for the induced linear order on the circle. We prove the uniqueness of the empirical expectile, as well as its consistency and finite-dimensional asymptotic normality. Finally, we indicate possible applications to circular measures of dispersion, skewness, and symmetry diagnostics.

</details>


### [282] [A scalable version of MADD for big-data classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08334)
*Annesha Ghosh,Adrija Saha,Soham Sarkar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维低样本量场景下的MADD半度量距离计算复杂度高的问题，本文提出一种可扩展的MADD版本，通过选择代表性子集和利用随机傅里叶特征显著降低计算复杂度，同时保持与原始MADD相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏距离在高维空间中面临距离集中、邻域结构破坏和hub现象等问题。MADD半度量能有效应对这些高维问题，但其计算复杂度随训练样本量呈二次增长，导致在大样本高维数据集上计算成本过高，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出两种加速策略：(1) 通过选取代表性子集（representative set）来减少MADD计算中涉及的样本对数量，从而降低复杂度；(2) 当样本量极大时，进一步利用随机傅里叶特征（Random Fourier Features）实现额外加速。

Result: 从理论和数值实验两方面证明了所提方法能够在仅需MADD计算时间的一小部分的情况下，达到与原始MADD相近的分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的可扩展MADD方法成功将MADD的应用范围拓展到具有大量观测样本的大数据场景，在保持高维分类优势的同时大幅提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Distance-based classifiers are very popular, and the Euclidean distance is one of the most commonly used metrics in distance-based classifiers. However, classifiers based on the Euclidean distance often suffer in high-dimensional setups due to issues such as distance concentration, violation of neighborhood structures, and the presence of hubs. In high-dimension, low-sample-size (HDLSS) situations, a data-driven semi-metric called the Mean Absolute Difference of Distances (MADD) is known to circumvent these issues. But one major problem with MADD is that its computational complexity increases quadratically with the training sample size. As a result, the application of MADD becomes computationally challenging for big datasets that have both a high dimension as well as a large number of observations. In this paper, we propose a scalable version of MADD that significantly reduces its computational complexity while retaining its advantages. This speed-up is achieved by selecting a representative set during the computation of MADD. Further speed-ups are achieved by using the idea of Random Fourier Features, particularly when the sample size is very large. We establish that our proposed methods achieve performances similar to MADD but only at a fraction of its computing time, both theoretically as well as numerically. Our approach broadens the scope of MADD, allowing its use to big-data with a very large number of observations.

</details>


### [283] [Testing Covariance Separability in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08388)
*Tomas Masak,Marcus Mayrhofer,Una Radojičić*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高维设定下检验矩阵变量数据协方差可分离性的新方法，通过将问题转化为白化后的球形检验，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟进行校准，同时引入角度版本以减少分布假设依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可分离性是矩阵变量数据协方差结构的重要假设，能极大简化协方差相关统计任务的解释与计算，但在高维情形下检验该假设十分困难，现有方法存在不足。

Method: 首先利用可分离协方差的最大似然估计对数据进行白化，将可分离性检验转化为球形检验；通过蒙特卡洛模拟对检验进行校准以实现有限样本水平控制；进一步引入基于白化后径向归一化的角度版本检验，降低对分布假设的依赖。

Result: 证明了在稠密备择假设下检验的高维相合性；通过模拟和实际数据展示了该方法在有限样本下的水平控制、经验功效和计算效率方面的良好表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于白化后球形检验的框架能够有效处理高维矩阵变量数据的可分离性检验问题，角度版本进一步增强了方法的鲁棒性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Separability is an important structural assumption often placed on the covariance when working with matrix-variate data, because it greatly simplifies both interpretation and computation of subsequent covariance-based statistical tasks. Yet testing the separability assumption is difficult in the high-dimensional regime. We propose to test separability by recasting the problem as a sphericity test after whitening the data using the separable maximum likelihood estimate of the covariance. The test is calibrated by Monte Carlo simulation, yielding finite-sample level control. Furthermore, we prove the test's high-dimensional consistency under dense alternatives. To reduce its reliance on distributional assumptions, we introduce an angular version of the test based on radial normalization after whitening. We demonstrate the practical utility, empirical power, and computational efficiency of the prop

</details>


### [284] [Bayesian DAG Structure Learning with Simultaneous Shrinkage Covariance Estimation under Scale-Mixture Error Distributions in the Proportional High-Dimensional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08428)
*Samaneh Nazari,Mohammad Arashi,Abdolnasser Sadeghkhani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出R-DACH框架，在高维比例渐近机制下，通过DAG-Cholesky因子的horseshoe先验实现DAG结构学习与精度矩阵估计，并利用逐观测逆伽马尺度混合获得对重尾和污染数据的鲁棒性，设计部分塌陷分块Gibbs采样器，模拟和TCGA RNA-seq数据验证其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DAG结构学习方法在高维情形下对重尾分布和污染观测敏感，且稀疏性先验与拓扑排序一致性难以统一。需要在比例渐近机制(p/n→c)下同时实现：1) 一致的父集选择；2) 对异常值的自动鲁棒性；3) 可扩展的后验推断。

Method: 1) 将全局-局部horseshoe先验直接置于DAG-Markov精度矩阵的修正Cholesky因子的严格下三角元素上，使Cholesky参数的稀疏性诱导与变量拓扑排序一致的父集选择；2) 引入逐观测逆伽马尺度混合，将正态误差推广至Student-t、Laplace、slash等重尾分布；3) 设计部分塌陷的分块Gibbs采样器，在排序、稀疏模式和连续参数的联合空间上进行后验探索。

Result: 模拟实验覆盖p达数百的多种(n,p)配置，验证了理论速率；在污染数据下，相比graphical-horseshoe、DAG-Wishart和基于PC算法的方法，R-DACH在图结构恢复和精度矩阵估计上均有显著提升。TCGA RNA-seq数据应用中发现了具有生物学可解释性的调控结构，而竞争方法未能恢复这些结构。

Conclusion: R-DACH通过统一的贝叶斯框架，将DAG结构学习、精度矩阵估计和鲁棒建模整合于比例渐近机制下，兼具理论保证和实用价值，尤其适用于生物医学等高噪声高维数据场景。

Abstract: We propose a unified Bayesian framework namely robust DAG-Cholesky horseshoe (R-DACH) for joint directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning and precision matrix estimation in the high-dimensional proportional asymptotic regime $p/n \to c \in (0,\infty)$, under the scale mixture of normal errors. The construction places a global-local horseshoe-type prior directly on the strictly lower-triangular entries of the modified Cholesky factor of the DAG-Markov precision matrix, so that sparsity in the Cholesky parameters induces a coherent parent-set selection consistent with a topological ordering of the variables. A per-observation inverse-gamma scale mixture yields automatic robustness to heavy-tailed and contaminated observations and admits Student-$t$, Laplace, and slash distributions as special cases. We design a partially-collapsed blocked Gibbs sampler that traverses the joint space of orderings, sparsity patterns and continuous parameters. Simulations across $(n,p)$ configurations with $p$ up to several hundreds confirm the theoretical rates and demonstrate substantial gains over graphical-horseshoe, DAG-Wishart, and PC-based competitors under contamination. An application to RNA-seq gene-expression data from \emph{The Cancer Genome Atlas} reveals biologically interpretable regulatory structure that competing methods fail to recover.

</details>


### [285] [weightflow: declarative, recipe-aware survey weighting in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08491)
*Juan Pablo Ferreira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: weightflow 是一个基于 R 语言的依赖无关包，通过声明式、可管道化的 API 将复杂调查权重的整个层级调整流程构建为单一可审计对象，并利用重抽样自助法和刀切法将每一阶段的不确定性传播至复制权重中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 R 工具仅覆盖复杂调查权重构建工作流的部分环节，缺乏将整个层级调整流程表达为单一可审计对象的能力，且无法将每一调整阶段（未知资格判定、非响应校正、校准等）的变异性传播至复制权重中，导致方差估计遗漏中间步骤的不确定性。

Method: 提出 weightflow 包：采用声明式、管道化的 tidymodels 风格 API，通过 step_*() 链定义权重配方（recipe），用 prep() 进行估计，实现定义与应用的分离。支持 raking、后分层、线性/GREG 校准（含边界约束、岭惩罚、域分区及积分变体）、Wu-Sitter 模型辅助校准、加权组与机器学习响应倾向校正（含交叉拟合）、R 指示量等。通过重抽样自助法和删除一个初级抽样单元的刀切法，在每次复制时重新应用整个配方，使复制权重携带所有估计阶段的不确定性。权重与复制权重可桥接至 survey 和 srvyr 包进行基于设计的推断。

Result: 校准和方差结果与 survey 包进行了验证；在捆绑样本和乌拉圭 ECH 住户调查微观数据上演示了完整级联流程，成功恢复了已知贫困率并给出了基于设计的不确定性。

Conclusion: weightflow 填补了复杂调查权重分析工作流中整体可审计性和全阶段方差传播的空白，为基于设计的推断提供了端到端的透明解决方案。

Abstract: Producing analysis weights for a complex survey requires a sequence of hierarchical adjustments (resolving unknown eligibility, dropping out-of-scope units, restoring within-household selection, correcting for nonresponse, and calibrating to known population totals), after which design-consistent variances must account for the fact that several adjustments were themselves estimated from the sample. Existing R tools cover parts of this workflow, but none expresses the whole cascade as a single auditable object, nor propagates the variability of every stage into the replicate weights. We present weightflow, a dependency-free (base R) package that builds survey weights through a declarative, pipeable, tidymodels-style API: a recipe is defined lazily as a chain of step_*() adjustments and estimated with prep(). Separating definition from application makes the process reproducible and auditable, and lets a rescaling bootstrap and a delete-a-PSU jackknife re-apply the entire recipe on each replicate, so the replicate weights carry the uncertainty of every estimated stage, not only of the final calibration. The package implements raking, post-stratification and linear/GREG calibration (with bounds, ridge penalisation, and domain-partitioned and integrative variants), model-assisted (Wu-Sitter) calibration, weighting-class and machine-learning response-propensity adjustments with cross-fitting, and representativity (R-)indicators. Weights and replicate weights bridge to the survey and srvyr packages for design-based inference. We validate the calibration and variance results against survey, and illustrate the full cascade on a bundled sample and on real household-survey microdata (the Uruguayan ECH), where it recovers a known poverty rate with design-based uncertainty.

</details>


### [286] [Calibrated Persistent-Laplacian CUSUM for Online Change-Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08635)
*Shiheng Nie,Yunguang Yue*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出PL-CUSUM方法，将持久拉普拉斯谱与Page-CUSUM递归结合，用于高维非线性时间序列在线变点检测，在理论和实验上均展现出良好的误报控制与检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于持久同调的变点检测方法仅使用拓扑摘要（如条形码、持续性图），丢失了谱信息中蕴含的尺度内连通性和几何结构信息。作者希望利用持久拉普拉斯谱来弥补这一不足，提升对高维非线性时间序列变点的检测能力。

Method: 提出PL-CUSUM方法：(1)将滑动窗口数据转化为点云；(2)计算持久拉普拉斯谱作为监测统计量；(3)将谱信息输入Page-CUSUM递归框架构建累积和检测器；(4)设计Phase I/Phase II两阶段流程——Phase I进行参数选择和阈值校准，Phase II进行在线递归检测。理论上推导了oracle检测器的误报延迟界，并证明plug-in白化得分在有限监测范围内仍能控制误报。

Result: 理论分析给出了oracle检测器的误报-延迟界，并证明plug-in白化得分可在有限监测窗口内控制误报。在仿真系统与真实监测数据上的实验表明，PL-CUSUM提供稳定的误报控制，且检测性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PL-CUSUM通过融合持久拉普拉斯谱与Page-CUSUM框架，有效捕获了超越同调计数的尺度内几何结构信息，在理论上具有可控的误报与检测延迟性能，实验中亦验证了其稳定性和竞争力。

Abstract: We propose the Persistent Laplacian Cumulative Sum (PL-CUSUM), an online change-point detection method for high-dimensional nonlinear time series. The method converts sliding windows into point clouds and uses persistent Laplacian spectra to construct the monitoring score for the Page cumulative sum (Page-CUSUM) recursion. Compared with detectors based only on persistent-homology summaries, PL-CUSUM further uses spectral information to capture within-scale connectivity and geometric structure beyond homology counts. Theoretically, we analyze two key performance criteria: false-alarm control and detection delay. We derive false-alarm-delay bounds for the oracle detector and show that the plug-in whitened score still controls false alarms over a finite monitoring horizon. Methodologically, we provide a Phase I/Phase II procedure that performs parameter selection and control-limit calibration before online recursion. Experiments on simulated systems and real monitoring data show that PL-CUSUM provides stable false-alarm control and competitive detection performance.

</details>


### [287] [Severity estimation in dependent collective risk models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08719)
*Christopher Blier-Wong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当索赔次数与索赔强度存在相依性时，汇集索赔数据并非来自边际强度分布，而是收敛于一个规模偏倚的混合分布，并基于此构建了复合似然估计方法，在Sarmanov模型下验证了其一致性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保险精算中，集体风险模型通常假设索赔次数与索赔强度独立，但现实中二者常存在相依性。当存在相依时，直接对汇集索赔数据拟合边际强度分布会导致不一致估计，因此需要识别汇集索赔真实服从的分布，并建立一致的参数估计方法。

Method: 首先证明汇集索赔的经验分布收敛于"任意观测索赔"的分布——即条件强度分布的规模偏倚混合分布；然后基于该分布构建复合似然估计方法，以保单（而非索赔）作为抽样单元；在Sarmanov集体风险模型下，推导出观测索赔密度和聚合均值的闭合形式，并利用Godambe信息矩阵建立渐近正态性。

Result: 证明了任何直接对汇集索赔拟合边际强度分布的方法在相依情形下不一致；复合似然估计能够校正这种偏差；模拟研究量化了朴素汇集拟合的偏差大小、复合似然的校正效果，以及基于保单层面的标准误的覆盖率表现。

Conclusion: 在索赔次数与强度相依的集体风险模型中，汇集索赔服从规模偏倚混合分布而非边际分布；基于该分布构建的复合似然估计具有一致性和渐近正态性，以保单为抽样单元的标准误能够提供正确的推断，有效解决了传统方法的不一致问题。

Abstract: The collective risk model represents the aggregate loss of an insurance portfolio as a random sum of individual claim severities. When claim counts and severities are dependent, the claims pooled across policies are no longer a sample from the marginal severity distribution. We show that their empirical distribution converges to the law of an arbitrary observed claim, a size-biased mixture of the conditional severity distributions, so any procedure that fits the severity margin directly to pooled claims is inconsistent in general. The same result identifies the distribution that the pooled claims do sample, and we build a composite likelihood estimation procedure on that distribution. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality, with Godambe information in which the policy, rather than the claim, is the sampling unit. In a Sarmanov collective risk model, the observed-claim density and the aggregate mean are in closed form. A simulation study measures the bias of naive pooled-severity fitting, its correction by the composite likelihood, and the coverage of the policy-level standard errors.

</details>


### [288] [On improving the estimates of the sampling variances via Global-Local priors in Small Area Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08720)
*Sirapat Watakajaturaphon,Jairo Fúquene-Patiño*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对Fay-Herriot模型中抽样方差被错误假定为已知的问题，本文提出一种新的贝叶斯模型，利用Global-Local先验来改进小域估计中抽样方差的后验估计，通过自适应MCMC算法解决计算难题，并在模拟和实际数据中验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fay-Herriot模型将抽样方差视为已知，但实际中抽样方差是从数据估计得到的，且往往高度不稳定。尽管广义方差函数（GVF）常用于跨区域借力以稳定估计，但仍存在改进空间。本文旨在通过贝叶斯框架更精确地对抽样方差进行后验估计，解决小域估计中这一长期困扰实际应用的核心问题。

Method: 提出一个分层贝叶斯模型，核心创新在于引入Global-Local（GL）先验分布来调节向GVF后验估计的收缩程度。具体地，GL先验通过全局参数控制整体收缩强度、局部参数允许各域自适应调整，从而在稳定估计与保留域间异质性之间取得平衡。针对模型中涉及Gamma函数的条件分布带来的计算困难，开发了自适应MCMC算法进行高效后验采样。同时，论文还研究了所提模型的理论性质。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提贝叶斯模型在抽样方差估计的精度和稳定性上均优于传统FH模型及其他竞争方法。在两个实际应用中——美国农业部玉米产量估计和哥伦比亚市级教育成就指数流行率估计——该方法均表现出更好的估计性能，验证了其在实际小域估计问题中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 通过在FH框架中融入Global-Local先验对抽样方差进行贝叶斯建模，能够有效解决抽样方差估计不稳定的问题。该方法兼具理论保证和计算可行性，为官方统计中的小域估计提供了一种更为可靠的工具，具有推广价值。

Abstract: The Fay-Herriot (FH) model is widely used in official statistics to produce reliable estimates for domains with small sample sizes. In the classical FH model, the sampling variances are treated as known, even though they are typically estimated from the data. In practice, these variance estimates can be highly variable. To address this issue, practitioners often use Generalized Variance Functions (GVFs) to borrow strength across areas and stabilize estimation. In this work, we propose a new Bayesian model to improve the posterior estimation of the sampling variances in Small Area Estimation (SAE). Our proposed model incorporates Global-Local (GL) priors to improve the level of shrinkage toward the posterior estimates obtained with the GVF function. We study the theoretical properties of the proposed model and develop adaptive Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms to address computational challenges arising from conditional distributions involving Gamma functions. The performance of the proposed Bayesian model is investigated through simulation studies and compared with competing approaches. Finally, we implement our proposal in two real applications by estimating the Corn production from the U.S. Department of Agriculture and the Prevalence of the Educational Attainment Index at the municipality levels in Colombia.

</details>
